
cge_clear_capture_temp_data = {
	clear_variable = cge_cap_char_key
	clear_variable = cge_cap_name_order
	clear_variable = cge_cap_rank
	clear_variable = cge_cap_state
	clear_variable = cge_cap_level
	clear_variable = cge_cap_attack
	clear_variable = cge_cap_defense
	clear_variable = cge_cap_planning
	clear_variable = cge_cap_logistics
	clear_variable = cge_cap_capacity
	clear_variable = cge_cap_attack_bonus
	clear_variable = cge_cap_defense_bonus
	clear_variable = cge_cap_planning_bonus
	clear_variable = cge_cap_logistics_bonus
	clear_variable = cge_cap_trait_count
	clear_variable = cge_tmp_trait_1
	clear_variable = cge_tmp_trait_2
	clear_variable = cge_tmp_trait_3
	clear_variable = cge_tmp_trait_4
	clear_variable = cge_tmp_trait_5
	clear_variable = cge_tmp_trait_6
	clear_variable = cge_tmp_trait_7
	clear_variable = cge_tmp_trait_8
	clear_variable = cge_cap_prisoner_id
	clear_variable = cge_cap_portrait_key
}


cge_snapshot_live_stats_on_from = {
	event_target:cge_capture_stats_captor = {
		set_variable = {
			var = cge_cap_attack_bonus
			value = cge_cap_attack
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			var = cge_cap_attack_bonus
			value = 2.5
		}

		set_variable = {
			var = cge_cap_defense_bonus
			value = cge_cap_defense
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			var = cge_cap_defense_bonus
			value = 2.5
		}

		set_variable = {
			var = cge_cap_planning_bonus
			value = cge_cap_planning
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			var = cge_cap_planning_bonus
			value = 2.5
		}

		set_variable = {
			var = cge_cap_logistics_bonus
			value = cge_cap_logistics
		}
		multiply_variable = {
			var = cge_cap_logistics_bonus
			value = 2.5
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			is_field_marshal = yes
		}

		event_target:cge_capture_stats_captor = {
			set_variable = {
				cge_cap_rank = 2
			}
			set_variable = {
				cge_cap_capacity = 5
			}
		}
	}
	else = {
		event_target:cge_capture_stats_captor = {
			set_variable = {
				cge_cap_rank = 1
			}
			set_variable = {
				cge_cap_capacity = 24
			}
		}
	}
}

cge_identify_character_on_from = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_variable = cge_registry_character_key }
				NOT = { has_variable = cge_registry_name_order }
			}
		}
		cge_identify_character_on_from_base = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = cge_registry_character_key }
		event_target:cge_capture_stats_captor = {
			set_variable = { var = cge_cap_char_key value = PREV.cge_registry_character_key }
			set_variable = { cge_cap_name_order = 1000000 }
			if = {
				limit = { PREV = { has_variable = cge_registry_name_order } }
				set_variable = { var = cge_cap_name_order value = PREV.cge_registry_name_order }
			}
		}
	}
}

cge_append_capture_trait_id = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = cge_cap_trait_count value = 8 compare = less_than } }
		meta_effect = {
			text = { set_variable = { cge_tmp_trait_[CGE_POS] = [CGE_ID] } }
			CGE_POS = "[?cge_cap_trait_pos|.0]"
			CGE_ID = "[?cge_cap_trait_id|.0]"
		}
		add_to_variable = { cge_cap_trait_count = 1 }
	}
	clear_variable = cge_cap_trait_pos
	clear_variable = cge_cap_trait_id
}

cge_snapshot_held_state_on_from = {

cge_prepare_capture_snapshot_on_from = {
	FROM = {
		cge_clear_capture_temp_data = yes
		save_event_target_as = cge_capture_stats_captor
		clr_country_flag = cge_capture_actual_leader_found
	}

	cge_snapshot_live_traits_on_from = yes
	cge_snapshot_held_state_on_from = yes

	set_unit_leader_flag = cge_capture_resolve_marker

	OWNER = {
		random_army_leader = {
			include_invisible = yes

			limit = {
				has_unit_leader_flag = cge_capture_resolve_marker
			}

			clr_unit_leader_flag = cge_capture_resolve_marker
			save_event_target_as = cge_capture_actual_leader

			cge_identify_character_on_from = yes

			if = {
				limit = {
					event_target:cge_capture_stats_captor = {
						check_variable = {
							var = cge_cap_char_key
							value = 1
							compare = less_than
						}
					}
					NOT = {
						has_variable = cge_runtime_portrait_key
					}
				}

				cge_assign_owner_portrait = yes
			}

			if = {
				limit = {
					has_variable = cge_runtime_portrait_key
				}

				event_target:cge_capture_stats_captor = {
set_variable = {
						var = cge_cap_portrait_key
						value = PREV.cge_runtime_portrait_key
					}
				}
			}

			cge_capture_character_stats = yes
			cge_snapshot_live_stats_on_from = yes

			if = {
				limit = {
					event_target:cge_capture_stats_captor = {
						check_variable = {
							var = cge_cap_char_key
							value = 1
							compare = less_than
						}
					}
				}

				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							var = cge_runtime_prisoner_id
							value = 1
							compare = less_than
						}
					}

					event_target:cge_capture_stats_captor = {
						add_to_variable = {
							cge_runtime_prisoner_sequence = 1
						}
					}

					set_variable = {
						var = cge_runtime_prisoner_id
						value = event_target:cge_capture_stats_captor.cge_runtime_prisoner_sequence
					}
				}

				event_target:cge_capture_stats_captor = {
					set_variable = {
						var = cge_cap_prisoner_id
						value = PREV.cge_runtime_prisoner_id
					}

					set_variable = {
						cge_cap_name_order = 1000000
					}

					add_to_variable = {
						var = cge_cap_name_order
						value = cge_cap_prisoner_id
					}
				}
			}

			event_target:cge_capture_stats_captor = {
				set_country_flag = cge_capture_actual_leader_found
			}

			log = "CGE ARMY LEADER RESOLVED: name=[THIS.GetName] static_key=[?event_target:cge_capture_stats_captor.cge_cap_char_key|0] runtime_id=[?cge_runtime_prisoner_id|0] level=[?event_target:cge_capture_stats_captor.cge_cap_level|0]"
		}
	}

	clr_unit_leader_flag = cge_capture_resolve_marker

	FROM = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = cge_capture_actual_leader_found
				}
			}

			log = "CGE ERROR: random_army_leader could not recover the defeated leader."
		}
	}
}


cge_capture_character_stats = {
	event_target:cge_capture_stats_captor = {
		set_variable = { var = cge_cap_level value = PREV.skill_level }
		set_variable = { var = cge_cap_attack value = PREV.attack_skill_level }
		set_variable = { var = cge_cap_defense value = PREV.defense_skill_level }
		set_variable = { var = cge_cap_planning value = PREV.planning_skill_level }
		set_variable = { var = cge_cap_logistics value = PREV.logistics_skill_level }
		set_country_flag = cge_capture_stats_found
	}
}
